[0] 2026-06-01:
对首页的驾驶舱进行优化升级。
一、总体架构与布局 (Layout & Architecture)
布局模式： 采用经典的 三栏式布局。这种布局适合信息密度较高的大屏，能够清晰地将业务流分为“任务输入/源头”、“核心资源（人）”、“执行结果/监控”。
分辨率适配： 基于宽屏1920x1080 的宽屏设计。开发时需考虑使用 rem、vw/vh 或 CSS Grid/Flexbox 进行响应式适配，确保在不同比例的大屏上内容不溢出、不变形。
视觉风格： 深色科技风（Dark Mode）。背景色为深蓝/黑灰，文字和图表使用高亮色（青色、紫色、橙色）以突出数据重点。
二、模块详细设计与需求拆解
1. 顶部导航与全局控制栏
功能定义： 全局筛选器与系统入口。
组件需求：
标题区： 居中显示“绩效分析评估系统驾驶舱”。
筛选器组： 左侧放置下拉选择框，包括“考核年度”（默认2026年）、“考核方案”、“考核分组”。这些是全局状态变量，改变后需触发下方所有图表的数据重新请求。
操作区： 右侧放置“镇街评价”按钮和“综合搜索”输入框。
2. 左侧面板：任务源与历史回溯
此区域主要关注“事”，即任务的来源构成和历史结果。
模块 A：任务解构管理
可视化组件： 韦恩图 或 多层环形图。
数据逻辑： 展示不同层级任务的数量对比。
核心数据：市级重点任务、仅市级重点任务、仅区级重点任务等交集数据。
底部统计：精准解构数、主动申报数。
交互需求： 鼠标悬停显示具体数值详情。
模块 B：2025年 考核结果管理
可视化组件： 数据表格。
数据字段： 排名、单位、姓名、考核得分、违法违纪组织处理、班子是否评优。
Tab切换： 右上角有“正职/副职”切换开关，点击切换表格数据源。
滚动需求： 如果数据量大，表格需要支持自动轮播滚动或手动滚动条。
3. 中间面板：干部画像（核心资源）
此区域是系统的核心，关注“人”，即被考核对象的详细画像。
模块 C：干部结构概览
可视化组件： 复合图表。
左侧：环形图 展示总人数。
右侧：条形图/列表 展示学历构成（全日制硕士研究生占比等）。
数据维度： 性别比例（男/女具体人数）、年龄段分布。
模块 D：政治与专业属性
可视化组件：
政治面貌：柱状图（中共党员 vs 民主党派 vs 无党派）。
民族：饼图。
专业分布：气泡图/词云图。圆圈大小代表人数多少。
擅长领域： 底部使用 矩形树图 或 色块拼接图。展示干部的特长分布，颜色区分不同类别。
4. 右侧面板：任务执行与流程监控
此区域关注“进度”和“流转”，即任务目前的执行状态。
模块 E：重点任务管理
可视化组件： 仪表盘/环形进度图。
核心指标： 任务流转总数。
分类统计： 党的建设等。
状态看板： 四个关键指标卡片 —— 已完成、达到时序进度、正在推进、推进滞后或未开展。每个卡片包含数量和百分比。
联动表格： 下方有一个明细表格，列出了具体的单位名称、干部姓名、序号、考核任务、完成情况、考核得分。这通常需要与上方的图表联动，或者作为详细数据的下钻展示。
模块 F：考核系统进度监控
可视化组件： 桑基图 或 流程节点图。
业务逻辑： 展示考核任务的生命周期流转。
节点定义： 考核办发布 -> 主考单位评分 -> 被考核单位自评 -> ... -> 主考单位评价任务。
视觉效果： 线条的粗细可能代表流转的任务数量，节点的高亮代表当前堆积的任务量。
三、开发技术栈建议
为了实现这个设计，建议使用以下技术栈：
前端框架： Vue.js 或 React。
图表库：
ECharts： 最适合此类大屏。它能完美实现中间的环形图、柱状图、气泡图，以及右侧的流程桑基图。
AntV G2/G6： 如果流程关系特别复杂，G6 是不错的选择。
UI 组件库： Element Plus 或 Ant Design Vue（用于顶部的下拉框、搜索框和表格）。
样式预处理： Sass/Less，配合 Tailwind CSS 快速构建网格布局。
数据交互： Axios 进行 API 请求，WebSocket 如果需要实时刷新“进度监控”模块的数据。
四、关键难点与注意事项
数据聚合逻辑： 中间面板的“干部结构”涉及多维度交叉。后端接口可能需要提供预聚合的数据，或者前端拿到原始数据后进行复杂的过滤计算。
字体清晰度： 在深色背景下，小字号的白色文字容易发虚。开发时需注意字体的抗锯齿渲染，或者适当加粗重要数字。
动态效果： 为了增加“驾驶舱”的感觉，建议给图表添加入场动画（如柱子生长、圆环绘制），给表格添加行高亮滚动效果。

“核心资源（人）”等模块，目前系统不具备，需要根据需求，进行功能设计和开发。
请根据系统原有功能和需开发的功能进行梳理和整合，我们沟通确认后，设计出执行方案，再进行执行开发。
保留之前的开发版本，便于随时撤回原版本，本次开发版本为1.2

[22] 2026-06-01:
This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - Port 考核评价系统 from Windows to 统信 UOS Linux with one-click startup, fixing CRLF line endings, watchdog compatibility, port conflicts, and sudo/pkexec integration
   - Add "清单管理" (Checklist Management) module for managing checklists associated with units
   - Upgrade dashboard to v1.2: dark-themed three-column cockpit with cadre profiles, task decomposition, flow monitoring, 2025 assessment results
   - Add "干部管理" (Cadre Management) module with full CRUD, import/export for individual cadres
   - Enhance task search with chip/tag-based visual filtering showing all preset dimensions vs. active ones
   - Implement four export types: current search, single-table all, by assessed unit ZIP, by assessor unit ZIP
   - Integrate "可反馈清单" text quality inspection engine into the system as "任务质检" module
   - Split quality check into two views: pending issues page + management dialog
   - Enable click-to-edit from quality issues, batch-correcting same task across units
   - Track issue status (pending/confirmed/resolved/ignored) with confirmed patterns feeding back to detection engine
   - Fix performance and UI issues in detection (pagination, loading states, white screen)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Flask + Vue 3 + Element Plus + SQLite stack
   - CRLF vs LF line endings (critical for Linux shell scripts)
   - `pkexec` vs `sudo` for privilege elevation on UOS (graphical password dialog)
   - `ss -tln` (no root needed) vs `ss -tlnp` (needs root for PIDs) for port detection
   - `fuser -k` for port cleanup
   - SQLAlchemy subquery-based filtering vs outerjoin (to avoid cross-contamination)
   - Jaccard bigram similarity for Chinese text duplicate detection
   - ECharts: sunburst, gauge, sankey, treemap, pie, bar charts with animations
   - MD5 hashing for confirmed pattern exclusion in detection engine
   - Pre-computed n-gram sets for O(n²) duplicate detection optimization
   - werkzeug/watchdog version incompatibility on UOS (Python 3.7 system packages)

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`tools/write_lf.py`** — Python script to write .sh files with LF endings, avoiding Windows CRLF
   - **`一键启动.sh`** — Root-level one-click startup for UOS/Linux; all fixes applied
   - **`linux/一键启动.sh`** — Same as root but with `DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"` for subdirectory
   - **`backend/models.py`** — Added Checklist, Cadre, AssessmentResult, QualityIssue, ConfirmedPattern models
     - `Checklist`: id, name, created_at + `checklist_unit` association table
     - `Cadre`: id, name, unit_id, gender, birth_date, education, fulltime_education, political_status, ethnicity, position_type, specialty, expertise
     - `AssessmentResult`: id, cadre_id, plan_year, position_type, total_score, has_violation, is_excellent, rank
     - `QualityIssue`: id, plan_id, task_id, issue_type, column_name, text, suggestion, context, confidence, status, created_at
     - `ConfirmedPattern`: id, issue_type, text_hash, created_at
   - **`backend/routes/checklist.py`** — Full CRUD + import/export/template endpoints
   - **`backend/routes/cadre.py`** — Cadre CRUD with 11-field import/export
   - **`backend/routes/assessment_result.py`** — Assessment result import/export/query
   - **`backend/routes/dashboard.py`** — Extended with 5 new cockpit APIs: cadre-stats, task-progress, flow, results, task-decomp
   - **`backend/services/dashboard_service.py`** — New service functions for cockpit data aggregation
   - **`backend/services/quality_checker.py`** — Full detection engine ported from `可反馈清单`:
     - `_check_typos()`: 80 homophone pairs + 4 shape confusions + year anomalies
     - `_check_logic()`: numbering inconsistency, contradictory scoring, broken sentences, vague quantifiers
     - `_check_punctuation()`: mixed CN/EN punctuation, missing endings, unpaired brackets
     - `_check_duplicates()`: exact match + Jaccard semantic similarity with pre-computed n-grams
     - Pre-indexed typo words (`_HOMOPHONE_INDEX`, `_SHAPE_INDEX`)
     - `_load_confirmed_hashes()`: preload all confirmed patterns to memory
     - `_hash_text()`: MD5 hashing for pattern matching
     - Critical fix: `iss["status"] = "pending"` added to all returned issues
   - **`backend/routes/quality_check.py`** — API endpoints:
     - `POST /api/quality-check/run` — run detection
     - `GET /api/quality-check/issues` — list with pagination and filtering
     - `PUT /api/quality-check/issues/` — update status (confirmed saves pattern, pending removes it)
     - `POST /api/quality-check/issues/batch-status` — batch status update
     - `POST /api/quality-check/batch-correct` — correct task and all siblings (same plan + assessor + key_work)
   - **`backend/routes/task.py`** — Extended list_tasks with `dimension_ids`, `key_works` params; fixed `assessor_unit_id`/`unit_id` to accept comma-separated strings; expanded "all" search to subquery-based multi-field search; four export endpoints
   - **`frontend/src/views/Dashboard.vue`** — Complete rewrite: dark-themed three-column cockpit with sunburst, gauge, sankey, treemap charts; ECharts animation; full-screen via Layout.vue `isCockpit` computed
   - **`frontend/src/views/Tasks.vue`** — Chip/tag filter system with preset+active merge; scoring_note column; search highlighting; multi-line text wrap; four-export dropdown
   - **`frontend/src/views/Checklists.vue`** — New checklist management page
   - **`frontend/src/views/Cadres.vue`** — New cadre management page with 11-field form
   - **`frontend/src/views/QualityCheck.vue`** — Detection page with full-page loading overlay, pagination, issue status actions, edit dialog with batch-correct alert, management dialog
   - **`frontend/src/views/IssueManage.vue`** — Created but later replaced by dialog in QualityCheck.vue
   - **`frontend/src/views/Layout.vue`** — Added `isCockpit` computed to hide sidebar/header on dashboard; added menu items for checklists, cadres, quality-check
   - **`frontend/src/router/index.js`** — Added routes for checklists, cadres, quality-check
   - **`frontend/src/style.css`** — Added cockpit dark theme CSS variables
   - **`frontend/src/api/checklist.js`**, **`frontend/src/api/cadre.js`** — API wrappers

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **CRLF line endings**: Shell scripts written on Windows with `\r\n` caused `#!/bin/bash\r` to fail on Linux. Fixed by creating `tools/write_lf.py` using `open(path, 'w', newline='\n')`.
   - **`linux/一键启动.sh` wrong path**: Written with same content as root script (no `cd ..`), causing it to run from `linux/` subdirectory instead of project root. Fixed by adding `/..` to DIR line.
   - **watchdog ImportError on UOS**: System `python3-watchdog` too old for new `werkzeug`. Fixed by adding `pip install --user --upgrade watchdog`.
   - **Port conflict (Address already in use)**: `ss -tlnp` without root doesn't show PIDs. Zombie Flask processes not killed. Fixed with `ss -tln` for detection + `fuser -k` for cleanup + `sudo` fallback.
   - **`sudo` password input not working on UOS terminal**: Terminal stdin not properly connected. Fixed by using `pkexec` (graphical password dialog) with `$ELEVATE`/`$ELEVATE_SH` helpers.
   - **Python version check too strict**: Required ≥3.8, UOS only has 3.7.3. Changed `-ge 8` to `-ge 7`.
   - **SQLAlchemy outerjoin causing wrong search results**: `outerjoin(Unit, db.or_(...))` caused cross-contamination in full-text search. Fixed by replacing with subquery-based approach using `Task.unit_id.in_(db.session.query(...))`.
   - **`assessor_unit_id` type=int breaking comma-separated IDs**: Frontend sends `"1,2,3"` but backend used `type=int`. Fixed by changing to string with manual `split(",")`.
   - **Vue SFC `}}` parsing error**: Arrow functions with `}}` like `(d=>({value:d.value,itemStyle:{color:'#xxx'}}))` confused Vue compiler. Fixed by using `function` syntax instead of arrow functions.
   - **Quality check returns no data (暂无数据)**: Raw issue dicts from detection engine lacked `status` field, causing frontend `filter(i => i.status === 'pending')` to filter everything. Fixed by adding `iss["status"] = "pending"` in `run_quality_check()`.
   - **White screen during detection**: `v-loading` on table only, with empty data, showed blank. Fixed with full-page `v-loading` overlay + pagination.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Successfully ported the entire system to UOS/Linux with self-contained one-click startup
   - Integrated `可反馈清单` detection engine into the system, adapting from pandas-based Excel processing to database Task objects
   - Optimized detection performance: pre-loaded confirmed hashes (avoiding 240 individual DB queries), pre-computed n-grams for duplicate comparisons, typo word indexing, length-based early filtering
   - Batch-correct feature: editing one task updates all siblings (same plan + assessor + key_work across different units)
   - Engine feedback loop: confirmed issues are hashed and excluded from future detection runs

8. Current Work:
   Fixing the quality check "暂无数据" issue. The root cause was that raw issue dicts from the detection engine functions (`_check_typos`, `_check_logic`, etc.) lacked a `status` field. The frontend's `filteredIssues` computed filters `arr.filter(i => i.status === 'pending')`, which was filtering out all results because the raw issue dicts had no `status` key. The `status` field only existed on the `QualityIssue` database model.

   Fixed by adding `iss["status"] = "pending"` to all returned issues in `run_quality_check()` in `backend/services/quality_checker.py`:
   ```python
   # 给每个 issue 加上 status 字段，前端筛选需要
   for iss in all_issues:
       iss["status"] = "pending"
   ```

   Committed as `395d3d0` with message "v1.2：修复检测结果无 status 字段导致前端显示空列表"

[29] 2026-06-01:
对首页的驾驶舱进行优化升级。
一、总体架构与布局 (Layout & Architecture)
布局模式： 采用经典的 三栏式布局。这种布局适合信息密度较高的大屏，能够清晰地将业务流分为“任务输入/源头”、“核心资源（人）”、“执行结果/监控”。
分辨率适配： 基于宽屏1920x1080 的宽屏设计。开发时需考虑使用 rem、vw/vh 或 CSS Grid/Flexbox 进行响应式适配，确保在不同比例的大屏上内容不溢出、不变形。
视觉风格： 深色科技风（Dark Mode）。背景色为深蓝/黑灰，文字和图表使用高亮色（青色、紫色、橙色）以突出数据重点。
二、模块详细设计与需求拆解
1. 顶部导航与全局控制栏
功能定义： 全局筛选器与系统入口。
组件需求：
标题区： 居中显示“绩效分析评估系统驾驶舱”。
筛选器组： 左侧放置下拉选择框，包括“考核年度”（默认2026年）、“考核方案”、“考核分组”。这些是全局状态变量，改变后需触发下方所有图表的数据重新请求。
操作区： 右侧放置“镇街评价”按钮和“综合搜索”输入框。
2. 左侧面板：任务源与历史回溯
此区域主要关注“事”，即任务的来源构成和历史结果。
模块 A：任务解构管理
可视化组件： 韦恩图 或 多层环形图。
数据逻辑： 展示不同层级任务的数量对比。
核心数据：市级重点任务、仅市级重点任务、仅区级重点任务等交集数据。
底部统计：精准解构数、主动申报数。
交互需求： 鼠标悬停显示具体数值详情。
模块 B：2025年 考核结果管理
可视化组件： 数据表格。
数据字段： 排名、单位、姓名、考核得分、违法违纪组织处理、班子是否评优。
Tab切换： 右上角有“正职/副职”切换开关，点击切换表格数据源。
滚动需求： 如果数据量大，表格需要支持自动轮播滚动或手动滚动条。
3. 中间面板：干部画像（核心资源）
此区域是系统的核心，关注“人”，即被考核对象的详细画像。
模块 C：干部结构概览
可视化组件： 复合图表。
左侧：环形图 展示总人数。
右侧：条形图/列表 展示学历构成（全日制硕士研究生占比等）。
数据维度： 性别比例（男/女具体人数）、年龄段分布。
模块 D：政治与专业属性
可视化组件：
政治面貌：柱状图（中共党员 vs 民主党派 vs 无党派）。
民族：饼图。
专业分布：气泡图/词云图。圆圈大小代表人数多少。
擅长领域： 底部使用 矩形树图 或 色块拼接图。展示干部的特长分布，颜色区分不同类别。
4. 右侧面板：任务执行与流程监控
此区域关注“进度”和“流转”，即任务目前的执行状态。
模块 E：重点任务管理
可视化组件： 仪表盘/环形进度图。
核心指标： 任务流转总数。
分类统计： 党的建设等。
状态看板： 四个关键指标卡片 —— 已完成、达到时序进度、正在推进、推进滞后或未开展。每个卡片包含数量和百分比。
联动表格： 下方有一个明细表格，列出了具体的单位名称、干部姓名、序号、考核任务、完成情况、考核得分。这通常需要与上方的图表联动，或者作为详细数据的下钻展示。
模块 F：考核系统进度监控
可视化组件： 桑基图 或 流程节点图。
业务逻辑： 展示考核任务的生命周期流转。
节点定义： 考核办发布 -> 主考单位评分 -> 被考核单位自评 -> ... -> 主考单位评价任务。
视觉效果： 线条的粗细可能代表流转的任务数量，节点的高亮代表当前堆积的任务量。
三、开发技术栈建议
为了实现这个设计，建议使用以下技术栈：
前端框架： Vue.js 或 React。
图表库：
ECharts： 最适合此类大屏。它能完美实现中间的环形图、柱状图、气泡图，以及右侧的流程桑基图。
AntV G2/G6： 如果流程关系特别复杂，G6 是不错的选择。
UI 组件库： Element Plus 或 Ant Design Vue（用于顶部的下拉框、搜索框和表格）。
样式预处理： Sass/Less，配合 Tailwind CSS 快速构建网格布局。
数据交互： Axios 进行 API 请求，WebSocket 如果需要实时刷新“进度监控”模块的数据。
四、关键难点与注意事项
数据聚合逻辑： 中间面板的“干部结构”涉及多维度交叉。后端接口可能需要提供预聚合的数据，或者前端拿到原始数据后进行复杂的过滤计算。
字体清晰度： 在深色背景下，小字号的白色文字容易发虚。开发时需注意字体的抗锯齿渲染，或者适当加粗重要数字。
动态效果： 为了增加“驾驶舱”的感觉，建议给图表添加入场动画（如柱子生长、圆环绘制），给表格添加行高亮滚动效果。

“核心资源（人）”等模块，目前系统不具备，需要根据需求，进行功能设计和开发。
请根据系统原有功能和需开发的功能进行梳理和整合，我们沟通确认后，设计出执行方案，再进行执行开发。
保留之前的开发版本，便于随时撤回原版本，本次开发版本为1.2

   Root cause: In `frontend/src/views/Dashboard.vue`, the `loadAll()` function called 6 dashboard APIs. The axios interceptor already extracts `res.data` from the HTTP response, so the variable already holds the JSON body `{"data": actualData}`. However, the code was doing a double `.data?.data` access (e.g., `cs.data?.data`), which on the first `.data` correctly extracted the inner data object, but the second `.data` returned `undefined` since the inner data object doesn't have a `data` property. The `|| fallbackValue` then caused all charts to use empty default values.

   Fix applied: Changed all five data access patterns from `X.data?.data || fallback` to `X.data || fallback` where X is cs, tp, fl, td and `rs.data || []` for results. Committed as `2070ace`.

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **CRLF line endings**: Shell scripts written on Windows with `\r\n` caused `#!/bin/bash\r` to fail on Linux. Fixed by creating `tools/write_lf.py` using `open(path, 'w', newline='\n')`.
   - **`linux/一键启动.sh` wrong path**: Written with same content as root script (no `cd ..`), causing it to run from `linux/` subdirectory instead of project root. Fixed by adding `/..` to DIR line.
   - **watchdog ImportError on UOS**: System `python3-watchdog` too old for new `werkzeug`. Fixed by adding `pip install --user --upgrade watchdog`.
   - **Port conflict (Address already in use)**: `ss -tlnp` without root doesn't show PIDs. Zombie Flask processes not killed. Fixed with `ss -tln` for detection + `fuser -k` for cleanup + `sudo` fallback.
   - **`sudo` password input not working on UOS terminal**: Terminal stdin not properly connected. Fixed by using `pkexec` (graphical password dialog) with `$ELEVATE`/`$ELEVATE_SH` helpers.
   - **Python version check too strict**: Required ≥3.8, UOS only has 3.7.3. Changed `-ge 8` to `-ge 7`.
   - **SQLAlchemy outerjoin causing wrong search results**: `outerjoin(Unit, db.or_(...))` caused cross-contamination in full-text search. Fixed by replacing with subquery-based approach using `Task.unit_id.in_(db.session.query(...))`.
   - **`assessor_unit_id` type=int breaking comma-separated IDs**: Frontend sends `"1,2,3"` but backend used `type=int`. Fixed by changing to string with manual `split(",")`.
   - **Vue SFC `}}` parsing error**: Arrow functions with `}}` like `(d=>({value:d.value,itemStyle:{color:'#xxx'}}))` confused Vue compiler. Fixed by using `function` syntax instead of arrow functions.
   - **Quality check returns no data (暂无数据)**: Raw issue dicts from detection engine lacked `status` field, causing frontend `filter(i => i.status === 'pending')` to filter everything. Fixed by adding `iss["status"] = "pending"` in `run_quality_check()`.
   - **White screen during detection**: `v-loading` on table only, with empty data, showed blank. Fixed with full-page `v-loading` overlay + pagination.

   - Batch-correct feature: editing one task updates all siblings (same plan + assessor + key_work across different units)

8. Current Work:
   Fixing the quality check "暂无数据" issue. The root cause was that raw issue dicts from the detection engine functions (`_check_typos`, `_check_logic`, etc.) lacked a `status` field. The frontend's `filteredIssues` computed filters `arr.filter(i => i.status === 'pending')`, which was filtering out all results because the raw issue dicts had no `status` key.    Fixed by adding `iss["status"] = "pending"` to all returned issues in `run_quality_check()` in `backend/services/quality_checker.py`:
   ```python
   # 给每个 issue 加上 status 字段，前端筛选需要
   for iss in all_issues:
       iss["status"] = "pending"
   ```

   Committed as `395d3d0` with message "v1.2：修复检测结果无 status 字段导致前端显示空列表"

   - Backend auto-auth: `desktop_auto_auth()` injects JWT token for requests missing Authorization header
   - All dashboard APIs return `jsonify({"data": actualData})` — interceptor strips to `{"data": actualData}`, frontend accesses `.data`
   - `build_task_query()` shared query builder with `search_type=main_task` support
   - QualityIssue statuses: `pending` → `confirmed` (false positive) / `resolved` (corrected) / `ignored`
   - ConfirmedPattern model for recording false positives to optimize detection logic
   - Jaccard bigram similarity for Chinese text duplicate detection
   - MD5 hashing for confirmed pattern detection
   - SQLAlchemy `joinedload()` for N+1 prevention
   - `URL.createObjectURL()` + temporary `` element for file downloads
   - Frontend served from `frontend/dist/` when running `python backend/app.py`; `.exe` is May 21 PyInstaller bundle (outdated)

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`frontend/src/views/Dashboard.vue`** — Main dashboard with 6 API calls (loadAll)
     - Fixed `decompStats.precise`/`declared` → `precise_count`/`declared_count` key mismatch (line 32-33)
     - Replaced `Promise.all` + `catch(_){}` with independent `safeCall()` pattern, each API fails independently
     - Updated initial ref: `const decompStats = ref({ precise_count:0, declared_count:0, total_count:0 })`
     ```js
     async function loadAll() {
       loading.value = true
       async function safeCall(key, fetcher) {
         try {
           const res = await fetcher()
           return { key, data: res?.data ?? null, ok: true }
         } catch (e) {
           console.error(`Dashboard ${key} 加载失败:`, e)
           return { key, data: null, ok: false }
         }
       }
       const results_arr = await Promise.all([
         safeCall('overview', () => getDashboardOverview(filterPlanId.value)),
         safeCall('cadreStats', () => http.get('/dashboard/cadre-stats')),
         safeCall('taskProgress', () => http.get('/dashboard/task-progress', { params: { plan_id: filterPlanId.value } })),
         safeCall('flow', () => http.get('/dashboard/flow', { params: { plan_id: filterPlanId.value } })),
         safeCall('results', () => http.get('/dashboard/results', { params: { plan_year: filterYear.value } })),
         safeCall('taskDecomp', () => http.get('/dashboard/task-decomp', { params: { plan_id: filterPlanId.value } })),
       ])
       for (const r of results_arr) {
         if (!r.ok || r.data === null) continue
         switch (r.key) {
           case 'overview': overview.value = r.data; break
           case 'cadreStats': cadreStats.value = r.data; break
           case 'taskProgress': taskProgress.value = r.data; break
           case 'flow': flowData.value = r.data; break
           case 'results': results.value = r.data; break
           case 'taskDecomp':
             decompData.value = r.data
             decompStats.value = r.data.stats || decompStats.value
             break
         }
       }
       charts()
       loading.value = false
     }
     ```

     - `loadIssues()` now recalculates summary stats from pending issues
     ```js
     async function loadIssues() {
       // ... fetches all issues
       const pending = items.filter(i => i.status === 'pending')
       issueTotal.value = pending.length
       if (summary.value) {
         summary.value.total_issues = pending.length
         const typeCounts = {}
         for (const cat of Object.keys(summary.value.summary)) { typeCounts[cat] = 0 }
         for (const i of pending) {
           for (const cat of Object.keys(typeCounts)) {
             const keyword = cat === '错别字' ? '错别字' : cat === '逻辑问题' ? '逻辑' : cat === '标点符号' ? '标点' : cat === '重复任务' ? '重复' : ''
             if (keyword && i.issue_type && i.issue_type.includes(keyword)) { typeCounts[cat]++ }
           }
         }
         summary.value.summary = typeCounts
       }
     }
     ```
     - Problem management uses grouped data: `mGrouped`, `mTotalConfirmed`, `mTotalResolved`
     - `undoGroup(group, type)` — batch reverts all items in a group back to pending

   - **`backend/routes/quality_check.py`** — New endpoints and fixes
     - `POST /api/quality-check/batch-confirm`: Marks all pending issues for same main_task as confirmed, creates ConfirmedPattern records
     ```python
     @qc_bp.route("/api/quality-check/batch-confirm", methods=["POST"])
     def batch_confirm():
         task = Task.query.get(task_id)
         siblings = Task.query.filter(
             Task.plan_id == task.plan_id,
             Task.assessor_unit_id == task.assessor_unit_id,
             Task.main_task == task.main_task,
         ).all()
         pending_issues = QualityIssue.query.filter(
             QualityIssue.task_id.in_(sibling_ids),
             QualityIssue.status == "pending",
         ).all()
         for iss in pending_issues:
             iss.status = "confirmed"
             if iss.text:
                 h = _hash_text(iss.text)
                 if not ConfirmedPattern.query.filter_by(issue_type=iss.issue_type, text_hash=h).first():
                     db.session.add(ConfirmedPattern(issue_type=iss.issue_type, text_hash=h))
         db.session.commit()
     ```
     - `POST /api/quality-check/correct-single`: Updates single task, resolves ALL pending issues for that task
     ```python
     @qc_bp.route("/api/quality-check/correct-single", methods=["POST"])
     def correct_single():
         for field, new_value in updates.items():
             if hasattr(task, field):
                 setattr(task, field, new_value)
         resolved = QualityIssue.query.filter(
             QualityIssue.task_id == task_id,
             QualityIssue.status == "pending",
         ).update({"status": "resolved"}, synchronize_session=False)
         db.session.commit()
     ```
     - `batch-correct`: Changed to handle `_noop` field, always resolves ALL pending issues for all siblings (removed column_name filter), added `main_task` filter
     ```python
     siblings_q = Task.query.filter(
         Task.plan_id == plan_id,
         Task.assessor_unit_id == assessor_id,
         Task.main_task == task.main_task,
     )
     if not is_noop and field and hasattr(Task, field):
         old_value = getattr(task, field, "")
         siblings_q = siblings_q.filter(getattr(Task, field) == old_value)
     siblings = siblings_q.all()
     # Always resolve all pending issues
     resolved = QualityIssue.query.filter(
         QualityIssue.task_id.in_([t.id for t in siblings]),
         QualityIssue.status == "pending",
     ).update({"status": "resolved"}, synchronize_session=False)
     ```
     - `GET /api/quality-check/issues/grouped`: Returns issues grouped by (main_task, assessor_name) with confirmed_items, resolved_items, unit_names, counts

[82] 2026-06-01:
对首页的驾驶舱进行优化升级。
一、总体架构与布局 (Layout & Architecture)
布局模式： 采用经典的 三栏式布局。这种布局适合信息密度较高的大屏，能够清晰地将业务流分为“任务输入/源头”、“核心资源（人）”、“执行结果/监控”。
分辨率适配： 基于宽屏1920x1080 的宽屏设计。开发时需考虑使用 rem、vw/vh 或 CSS Grid/Flexbox 进行响应式适配，确保在不同比例的大屏上内容不溢出、不变形。
视觉风格： 深色科技风（Dark Mode）。背景色为深蓝/黑灰，文字和图表使用高亮色（青色、紫色、橙色）以突出数据重点。
二、模块详细设计与需求拆解
1. 顶部导航与全局控制栏
功能定义： 全局筛选器与系统入口。
组件需求：
标题区： 居中显示“绩效分析评估系统驾驶舱”。
筛选器组： 左侧放置下拉选择框，包括“考核年度”（默认2026年）、“考核方案”、“考核分组”。这些是全局状态变量，改变后需触发下方所有图表的数据重新请求。
操作区： 右侧放置“镇街评价”按钮和“综合搜索”输入框。
2. 左侧面板：任务源与历史回溯
此区域主要关注“事”，即任务的来源构成和历史结果。
模块 A：任务解构管理
可视化组件： 韦恩图 或 多层环形图。
数据逻辑： 展示不同层级任务的数量对比。
核心数据：市级重点任务、仅市级重点任务、仅区级重点任务等交集数据。
底部统计：精准解构数、主动申报数。
交互需求： 鼠标悬停显示具体数值详情。
模块 B：2025年 考核结果管理
可视化组件： 数据表格。
数据字段： 排名、单位、姓名、考核得分、违法违纪组织处理、班子是否评优。
Tab切换： 右上角有“正职/副职”切换开关，点击切换表格数据源。
滚动需求： 如果数据量大，表格需要支持自动轮播滚动或手动滚动条。
3. 中间面板：干部画像（核心资源）
此区域是系统的核心，关注“人”，即被考核对象的详细画像。
模块 C：干部结构概览
可视化组件： 复合图表。
左侧：环形图 展示总人数。
右侧：条形图/列表 展示学历构成（全日制硕士研究生占比等）。
数据维度： 性别比例（男/女具体人数）、年龄段分布。
模块 D：政治与专业属性
可视化组件：
政治面貌：柱状图（中共党员 vs 民主党派 vs 无党派）。
民族：饼图。
专业分布：气泡图/词云图。圆圈大小代表人数多少。
擅长领域： 底部使用 矩形树图 或 色块拼接图。展示干部的特长分布，颜色区分不同类别。
4. 右侧面板：任务执行与流程监控
此区域关注“进度”和“流转”，即任务目前的执行状态。
模块 E：重点任务管理
可视化组件： 仪表盘/环形进度图。
核心指标： 任务流转总数。
分类统计： 党的建设等。
状态看板： 四个关键指标卡片 —— 已完成、达到时序进度、正在推进、推进滞后或未开展。每个卡片包含数量和百分比。
联动表格： 下方有一个明细表格，列出了具体的单位名称、干部姓名、序号、考核任务、完成情况、考核得分。这通常需要与上方的图表联动，或者作为详细数据的下钻展示。
模块 F：考核系统进度监控
可视化组件： 桑基图 或 流程节点图。
业务逻辑： 展示考核任务的生命周期流转。
节点定义： 考核办发布 -> 主考单位评分 -> 被考核单位自评 -> ... -> 主考单位评价任务。
视觉效果： 线条的粗细可能代表流转的任务数量，节点的高亮代表当前堆积的任务量。
三、开发技术栈建议
为了实现这个设计，建议使用以下技术栈：
前端框架： Vue.js 或 React。
图表库：
ECharts： 最适合此类大屏。它能完美实现中间的环形图、柱状图、气泡图，以及右侧的流程桑基图。
AntV G2/G6： 如果流程关系特别复杂，G6 是不错的选择。
UI 组件库： Element Plus 或 Ant Design Vue（用于顶部的下拉框、搜索框和表格）。
样式预处理： Sass/Less，配合 Tailwind CSS 快速构建网格布局。
数据交互： Axios 进行 API 请求，WebSocket 如果需要实时刷新“进度监控”模块的数据。
四、关键难点与注意事项
数据聚合逻辑： 中间面板的“干部结构”涉及多维度交叉。后端接口可能需要提供预聚合的数据，或者前端拿到原始数据后进行复杂的过滤计算。
字体清晰度： 在深色背景下，小字号的白色文字容易发虚。开发时需注意字体的抗锯齿渲染，或者适当加粗重要数字。
动态效果： 为了增加“驾驶舱”的感觉，建议给图表添加入场动画（如柱子生长、圆环绘制），给表格添加行高亮滚动效果。

“核心资源（人）”等模块，目前系统不具备，需要根据需求，进行功能设计和开发。
请根据系统原有功能和需开发的功能进行梳理和整合，我们沟通确认后，设计出执行方案，再进行执行开发。
保留之前的开发版本，便于随时撤回原版本，本次开发版本为1.2

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **CRLF line endings**: Shell scripts written on Windows with `\r\n` caused `#!/bin/bash\r` to fail on Linux. Fixed by creating `tools/write_lf.py` using `open(path, 'w', newline='\n')`.
   - **`linux/一键启动.sh` wrong path**: Written with same content as root script (no `cd ..`), causing it to run from `linux/` subdirectory instead of project root. Fixed by adding `/..` to DIR line.
   - **watchdog ImportError on UOS**: System `python3-watchdog` too old for new `werkzeug`. Fixed by adding `pip install --user --upgrade watchdog`.
   - **Port conflict (Address already in use)**: `ss -tlnp` without root doesn't show PIDs. Zombie Flask processes not killed. Fixed with `ss -tln` for detection + `fuser -k` for cleanup + `sudo` fallback.
   - **`sudo` password input not working on UOS terminal**: Terminal stdin not properly connected. Fixed by using `pkexec` (graphical password dialog) with `$ELEVATE`/`$ELEVATE_SH` helpers.
   - **Python version check too strict**: Required ≥3.8, UOS only has 3.7.3. Changed `-ge 8` to `-ge 7`.
   - **SQLAlchemy outerjoin causing wrong search results**: `outerjoin(Unit, db.or_(...))` caused cross-contamination in full-text search. Fixed by replacing with subquery-based approach using `Task.unit_id.in_(db.session.query(...))`.
   - **`assessor_unit_id` type=int breaking comma-separated IDs**: Frontend sends `"1,2,3"` but backend used `type=int`. Fixed by changing to string with manual `split(",")`.
   - **Vue SFC `}}` parsing error**: Arrow functions with `}}` like `(d=>({value:d.value,itemStyle:{color:'#xxx'}}))` confused Vue compiler. Fixed by using `function` syntax instead of arrow functions.
   - **Quality check returns no data (暂无数据)**: Raw issue dicts from detection engine lacked `status` field, causing frontend `filter(i => i.status === 'pending')` to filter everything. Fixed by adding `iss["status"] = "pending"` in `run_quality_check()`.
   - **White screen during detection**: `v-loading` on table only, with empty data, showed blank. Fixed with full-page `v-loading` overlay + pagination.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Successfully ported the entire system to UOS/Linux with self-contained one-click startup
   - Integrated `可反馈清单` detection engine into the system, adapting from pandas-based Excel processing to database Task objects
   - Optimized detection performance: pre-loaded confirmed hashes (avoiding 240 individual DB queries), pre-computed n-grams for duplicate comparisons, typo word indexing, length-based early filtering
   - Batch-correct feature: editing one task updates all siblings (same plan + assessor + key_work across different units)
   - Engine feedback loop: confirmed issues are hashed and excluded from future detection runs

8. Current Work:
   Fixing the quality check "暂无数据" issue. The root cause was that raw issue dicts from the detection engine functions (`_check_typos`, `_check_logic`, etc.) lacked a `status` field. The frontend's `filteredIssues` computed filters `arr.filter(i => i.status === 'pending')`, which was filtering out all results because the raw issue dicts had no `status` key. The `status` field only existed on the `QualityIssue` database model.

   Fixed by adding `iss["status"] = "pending"` to all returned issues in `run_quality_check()` in `backend/services/quality_checker.py`:
   ```python
   # 给每个 issue 加上 status 字段，前端筛选需要
   for iss in all_issues:
       iss["status"] = "pending"
   ```

   Committed as `395d3d0` with message "v1.2：修复检测结果无 status 字段导致前端显示空列表"

   Root cause: In `frontend/src/views/Dashboard.vue`, the `loadAll()` function called 6 dashboard APIs. The axios interceptor already extracts `res.data` from the HTTP response, so the variable already holds the JSON body `{"data": actualData}`. However, the code was doing a double `.data?.data` access (e.g., `cs.data?.data`), which on the first `.data` correctly extracted the inner data object, but the second `.data` returned `undefined` since the inner data object doesn't have a `data` property. The `|| fallbackValue` then caused all charts to use empty default values.

   Fix applied: Changed all five data access patterns from `X.data?.data || fallback` to `X.data || fallback` where X is cs, tp, fl, td and `rs.data || []` for results. Committed as `2070ace`.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Flask + SQLAlchemy + SQLite backend, Vue 3 + Element Plus frontend
   - 4 quality detection engines: `_check_typos` (homophone/shape confusions), `_check_logic` (text truncation, numbering, contradictions), `_check_punctuation` (missing periods, bracket mismatch), `_check_duplicates` (Jaccard n-gram similarity)
   - `ConfirmedPattern` model: stores (issue_type, text_hash) to auto-exclude confirmed false positives on re-run
   - `_is_confirmed_pattern()`: checks if detected issue matches a previously confirmed pattern
   - `existing_handled` set in `run_quality_check`: preloads confirmed/resolved issues to skip re-detection
   - `save_issues()` function: filters by ConfirmedPattern AND existing_handled before creating new pending QualityIssue records
   - Axios interceptor strips one level: returns `res.data` for JSON, full `res` for blob
   - Client-side pagination via `pagedIssues` computed property; `loadIssues` fetches all issues with page_size=2000
   - Race condition guard via `loadId` counter in `loadIssues()`
   - `batch-correct` backend supports both single-field (`field`+`new_value`) and multi-field (`updates: {field: new_value, ...}`) modes

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`backend/services/quality_checker.py`** — Core detection engine, most heavily modified
     - Added `joinedload` import and applied to Task query (N+1 fix)
     - Changed `all_issues` to `saved_orm_objects` list for proper DB id tracking
     - Made duplicates go through `save_issues()` (previously bypassed → never persisted)
     - Added `existing_handled` preload: queries confirmed/resolved issues and skips re-detection
     - Changed delete to `synchronize_session=False` + `expire_all()` to fix SAWarning
     - Expanded HOMOPHONE_CONFUSIONS from ~60 to ~200+ entries with three batches
     - Expanded SHAPE_CONFUSIONS from 4 to ~25 entries
     - **Final `run_quality_check` structure**:
     ```python
     def run_quality_check(plan_id=None):
         q = Task.query.options(joinedload(Task.unit), joinedload(Task.assessment_dimension), joinedload(Task.assessor_unit))
         if plan_id: q = q.filter(Task.plan_id == plan_id)
         tasks = q.all()
         rows = [_task_to_row(t) for t in tasks]
         
         if plan_id:
             QualityIssue.query.filter_by(plan_id=plan_id, status="pending").delete(synchronize_session=False)
             db.session.expire_all()
         
         confirmed_hashes = _load_confirmed_hashes()
         
         existing_handled = set()
         if plan_id:
             handled_rows = db.session.query(
                 QualityIssue.task_id, QualityIssue.issue_type,
                 QualityIssue.column_name, QualityIssue.text,
             ).filter(QualityIssue.plan_id == plan_id,
                      QualityIssue.status.in_(["confirmed", "resolved"])).all()
             for task_id, issue_type, col_name, text in handled_rows:
                 existing_handled.add((task_id, issue_type, col_name or "", (text or "")[:200]))
         
         saved_orm_objects = []
         def save_issues(issues_list):
             for iss in issues_list:
                 if _is_confirmed_pattern(iss, confirmed_hashes): continue
                 iss_key = (iss.get("task_id"), iss["issue_type"], iss.get("column", ""), (iss.get("text", "") or "")[:200])
                 if iss_key in existing_handled: continue
                 qi = QualityIssue(plan_id=plan_id, task_id=iss.get("task_id"),
                     issue_type=iss["issue_type"], column_name=iss.get("column", ""),
                     text=iss.get("text", "")[:500], suggestion=iss.get("suggestion", "")[:500],
                     context=iss.get("context", "")[:500], confidence=iss.get("confidence", "medium"),
                     status="pending")
                 db.session.add(qi)
                 saved_orm_objects.append((qi, iss))
         
         save_issues(_check_typos(rows))
         save_issues(_check_logic(rows))
         save_issues(_check_punctuation(rows))
         save_issues(_check_duplicates(rows))
         
         db.session.commit()
         
         all_issues = []
         for qi, original in saved_orm_objects:
             item = dict(original)
             item["id"] = qi.id
             item["status"] = "pending"
             all_issues.append(item)
         # ... statistics and return
     ```

   - **`backend/routes/quality_check.py`** — API endpoints

     ```

     ```javascript
     async function loadPlans() {
       try {
         const r = await getPlans();
         plans.value = r.data?.items || [];
         if (plans.value.length > 0) {
           const latest = plans.value.reduce((a, b) => (a.id > b.id ? a : b));
           filterPlanId.value = latest.id;
         }
       } catch (e) { console.error('加载方案失败:', e) }
     }
     ```

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - Axios HTTP client with response interceptor that returns `res.data` for JSON, full `res` for blob

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`frontend/src/views/Dashboard.vue`** — Main dashboard with 6 API calls (loadAll)
     - Fixed `decompStats.precise`/`declared` → `precise_count`/`declared_count` key mismatch (line 32-33)
     - Replaced `Promise.all` + `catch(_){}` with independent `safeCall()` pattern, each API fails independently
     - Updated initial ref: `const decompStats = ref({ precise_count:0, declared_count:0, total_count:0 })`
     ```js
     async function loadAll() {
       loading.value = true
       async function safeCall(key, fetcher) {
         try {
           const res = await fetcher()
           return { key, data: res?.data ?? null, ok: true }
         } catch (e) {
           console.error(`Dashboard ${key} 加载失败:`, e)
           return { key, data: null, ok: false }
         }
       }
       const results_arr = await Promise.all([
         safeCall('overview', () => getDashboardOverview(filterPlanId.value)),
         safeCall('cadreStats', () => http.get('/dashboard/cadre-stats')),
         safeCall('taskProgress', () => http.get('/dashboard/task-progress', { params: { plan_id: filterPlanId.value } })),
         safeCall('flow', () => http.get('/dashboard/flow', { params: { plan_id: filterPlanId.value } })),
         safeCall('results', () => http.get('/dashboard/results', { params: { plan_year: filterYear.value } })),
         safeCall('taskDecomp', () => http.get('/dashboard/task-decomp', { params: { plan_id: filterPlanId.value } })),
       ])
       for (const r of results_arr) {
         if (!r.ok || r.data === null) continue
         switch (r.key) {
           case 'overview': overview.value = r.data; break
           case 'cadreStats': cadreStats.value = r.data; break
           case 'taskProgress': taskProgress.value = r.data; break
           case 'flow': flowData.value = r.data; break
           case 'results': results.value = r.data; break
           case 'taskDecomp':
             decompData.value = r.data
             decompStats.value = r.data.stats || decompStats.value
             break
         }
       }
       charts()
       loading.value = false
     }
     ```

     ```js
     async function loadIssues() {
       // ... fetches all issues
       const pending = items.filter(i => i.status === 'pending')
       issueTotal.value = pending.length
       if (summary.value) {
         summary.value.total_issues = pending.length
         const typeCounts = {}
         for (const cat of Object.keys(summary.value.summary)) { typeCounts[cat] = 0 }
         for (const i of pending) {
           for (const cat of Object.keys(typeCounts)) {
             const keyword = cat === '错别字' ? '错别字' : cat === '逻辑问题' ? '逻辑' : cat === '标点符号' ? '标点' : cat === '重复任务' ? '重复' : ''
             if (keyword && i.issue_type && i.issue_type.includes(keyword)) { typeCounts[cat]++ }
           }
         }
         summary.value.summary = typeCounts
       }
     }
     ```
     - Problem management uses grouped data: `mGrouped`, `mTotalConfirmed`, `mTotalResolved`
     - `undoGroup(group, type)` — batch reverts all items in a group back to pending

   - **`backend/routes/quality_check.py`** — New endpoints and fixes
     - `POST /api/quality-check/batch-confirm`: Marks all pending issues for same main_task as confirmed, creates ConfirmedPattern records
     ```python
     @qc_bp.route("/api/quality-check/batch-confirm", methods=["POST"])
     def batch_confirm():
         task = Task.query.get(task_id)
         siblings = Task.query.filter(
             Task.plan_id == task.plan_id,
             Task.assessor_unit_id == task.assessor_unit_id,
             Task.main_task == task.main_task,
         ).all()
         pending_issues = QualityIssue.query.filter(
             QualityIssue.task_id.in_(sibling_ids),
             QualityIssue.status == "pending",
         ).all()
         for iss in pending_issues:
             iss.status = "confirmed"
             if iss.text:
                 h = _hash_text(iss.text)
                 if not ConfirmedPattern.query.filter_by(issue_type=iss.issue_type, text_hash=h).first():
                     db.session.add(ConfirmedPattern(issue_type=iss.issue_type, text_hash=h))
         db.session.commit()
     ```
     - `POST /api/quality-check/correct-single`: Updates single task, resolves ALL pending issues for that task
     ```python
     @qc_bp.route("/api/quality-check/correct-single", methods=["POST"])
     def correct_single():
         for field, new_value in updates.items():
             if hasattr(task, field):
                 setattr(task, field, new_value)
         resolved = QualityIssue.query.filter(
             QualityIssue.task_id == task_id,
             QualityIssue.status == "pending",
         ).update({"status": "resolved"}, synchronize_session=False)
         db.session.commit()
     ```
     - `batch-correct`: Changed to handle `_noop` field, always resolves ALL pending issues for all siblings (removed column_name filter), added `main_task` filter
     ```python
     siblings_q = Task.query.filter(
         Task.plan_id == plan_id,
         Task.assessor_unit_id == assessor_id,
         Task.main_task == task.main_task,
     )
     if not is_noop and field and hasattr(Task, field):
         old_value = getattr(task, field, "")
         siblings_q = siblings_q.filter(getattr(Task, field) == old_value)
     siblings = siblings_q.all()
     # Always resolve all pending issues
     resolved = QualityIssue.query.filter(
         QualityIssue.task_id.in_([t.id for t in siblings]),
         QualityIssue.status == "pending",
     ).update({"status": "resolved"}, synchronize_session=False)
     ```
     - `GET /api/quality-check/issues/grouped`: Returns issues grouped by (main_task, assessor_name) with confirmed_items, resolved_items, unit_names, counts

   - 4 detection engines: `_check_typos` (homophone/shape/year), `_check_logic` (numbering/contradiction/truncation/fuzzy), `_check_punctuation` (comma/bracket/missing end), `_check_duplicates` (Jaccard n-gram similarity)
   - `_hash_text()`: MD5 hashing for deterministic issue deduplication
   - `_load_confirmed_hashes(plan_id)`: loads ConfirmedPattern entries scoped to plan
   - `joinedload()`: SQLAlchemy eager loading to prevent N+1 queries
   - Client-side pagination via `pagedIssues` computed property
   - `loadId` counter for race condition protection in async operations
   - Chip/card filter pattern from Tasks.vue (chipCategory selector → chipOptions grid → toggleChip)
   - Vue 3 Composition API: ref, computed, reactive, watch
   - Axios interceptor: strips `.data` from response, so `r` is the body directly

     class ConfirmedPattern(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "confirmed_pattern"
         id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
         plan_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("plan.id"), nullable=False, comment="所属方案ID")
         issue_type = db.Column(db.String(50), nullable=False, comment="问题分类")
         text_hash = db.Column(db.String(64), nullable=False, comment="文本 MD5")

         __table_args__ = (
             db.UniqueConstraint("plan_id", "issue_type", "text_hash", name="uq_confirmed_pattern_plan_type_hash"),
         )
     ```

5. Problem Solving:
   - **240→210→209 root cause**: Confirmed as NOT a bug but a feature — ConfirmedPattern and existing_handled mechanisms intentionally skip previously handled issues. However, 3 bugs were found that could cause unexpected counting:

     2. text[:200] collision causing accidental dedup (fixed with MD5 hash)
     3. Non-deterministic duplicate detection (fixed with ORDER BY)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Vue 3 Composition API: `ref`, `reactive`, `computed`, `watch`, `nextTick`
   - Element Plus `el-tree` with `setCheckedKeys()`, `getCheckedNodes()`, `getHalfCheckedNodes()`
   - CSS overflow detection: `scrollHeight > clientHeight` to determine if content overflows a single line
   - `visibility: hidden` (not `display: none`) to preserve element space for alignment
   - `nextTick` for DOM-dependent measurements after reactive data changes
   - Flask + SQLAlchemy: copying DB records with ID mapping for foreign key relationships
   - CSS `max-height` + `overflow: hidden` for chip row collapsing

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`frontend/src/views/QualityCheck.vue`**
     - Chip toggle rework (template + script + CSS)
     - Template: Toggle moved before v-for, always rendered with `chip-toggle-hidden` class when not needed, removed `idx  setChipGridRef(dim.key, el)" :class="['chip-grid', { collapsed: dim.collapsed }]">
       {{ dim.collapsed ? '▼' : '▲' }}
       暂无数据
       
         {{ item.name || item }}
       
     
     ```
     ```javascript
     const chipGridRefs = {}
     function setChipGridRef(key, el) { if (el) chipGridRefs[key] = el }
     function checkChipOverflow() {
       chipDimensions.forEach(d => d.collapsed = true)
       nextTick(() => {
         for (const dim of chipDimensions) {
           const el = chipGridRefs[dim.key]
           if (el) {
             const overflows = el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight + 2
             dim.needsToggle = overflows
             dim.collapsed = overflows
           }
         }
       })
     }
     ```
     ```css
     .chip-grid.collapsed { max-height: 30px; overflow-y: hidden; }
     .chip-toggle-hidden { visibility: hidden; pointer-events: none; }
     ```
     - Added `nextTick` import, `needsToggle: false` to each chip dimension
     - Calls `checkChipOverflow()` after `buildChipOptions()` in both `loadIssues()` and `runCheck()`

   - **`frontend/src/views/Tasks.vue`**
     - Identical chip toggle changes as QualityCheck.vue
     - Same `checkChipOverflow()`, template, CSS changes
     - `checkChipOverflow()` called after `buildChipOptions()` in `loadFilterOpts()`

   - **`backend/routes/plan.py`**
     - Added `POST /api/plans//copy` endpoint (after line 184, `issue_plan`)
     - Copies plan config: creates new plan with name "原名称 - 副本" (with timestamp fallback for duplicates), copies evaluation_dimensions → assessment_dimensions (with `ad_id_map`), copies assessor_units, copies assessed_groups → units → dimension_weights (using ID mapping)
     ```python
     @plan_bp.route("/api/plans//copy", methods=["POST"])
     @jwt_required()
     def copy_plan(plan_id):
         src = Plan.query.get(plan_id)
         if not src: return jsonify({"msg": "方案不存在"}), 404
         copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本"
         existing = Plan.query.filter_by(name=copy_name).first()
         if existing: copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本({datetime.utcnow().strftime('%m%d%H%M')})"
         new_plan = Plan(name=copy_name, year=src.year, start_date=src.start_date,
                         end_date=src.end_date, created_date=datetime.utcnow().date(), status="draft")
         db.session.add(new_plan); db.session.flush()
         ad_id_map = {}
         for ed in src.evaluation_dimensions:
             new_ed = EvaluationDimension(name=ed.name, score=ed.score, is_actual_assessment=ed.is_actual_assessment,
                                          is_bonus_deduction=ed.is_bonus_deduction, sort_order=ed.sort_order, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_ed); db.session.flush()
             for ad in ed.assessment_dimensions:
                 new_ad = AssessmentDimension(name=ad.name, sort_order=ad.sort_order, evaluation_dimension_id=new_ed.id)
                 db.session.add(new_ad); db.session.flush()
                 ad_id_map[ad.id] = new_ad.id
         new_plan.assessor_units = src.assessor_units
         for g in src.assessed_groups:
             new_group = AssessedGroup(name=g.name, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_group); db.session.flush()
             new_group.units = g.units
             for gw in g.dimension_weights:
                 new_ad_id = ad_id_map.get(gw.assessment_dimension_id)
                 if new_ad_id:
                     db.session.add(GroupDimensionWeight(group_id=new_group.id, assessment_dimension_id=new_ad_id, weight=gw.weight))
         db.session.commit()
         return jsonify({"data": {"id": new_plan.id, "name": new_plan.name}, "msg": f"方案复制成功：{new_plan.name}"})
     ```

   - **`frontend/src/api/plan.js`**
     - Added `export function copyPlan(id) { return http.post(`/plans/${id}/copy`) }`

     - Added `checkedAssessorUnits` ref and tag list display below tree
     - Updated `onAssessorCheck()` to collect checked + half-checked unit nodes
     ```javascript
     const checkedAssessorUnits = ref([])
     function onAssessorCheck(_node, info) {
       checkedAssessorUnits.value = [...info.checkedNodes, ...info.halfCheckedNodes].filter(n => n.type === 'unit')
     }
     ```
     - Template addition below tree:
     ```html
     
       已选主考单位（{{ checkedAssessorUnits.length }}）：
       {{ u.name }}
     
     ```
     - `openDetail()` and `refreshDetail()` also initialize `checkedAssessorUnits.value = detail.value.assessor_units`

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Overflow detection always returning false**: `checkChipOverflow()` measured `scrollHeight > clientHeight` in the natural (non-collapsed) state where `max-height: 120px` fit all content. Fix: Force `collapsed = true` before measurement, then determine actual overflow.
   - **Class binding blocking collapsed style**: `{ collapsed: dim.needsToggle && dim.collapsed }` meant that when `needsToggle` was false (uninitialized), setting `collapsed = true` for measurement didn't apply the CSS class. Fix: Simplified to `{ collapsed: dim.collapsed }`.
   - **Second row peeking through**: `max-height: 36px` was too tall for one row (~28px chip height), allowing ~8px of second row visible. Fix: Reduced to `max-height: 30px`.
   - **Assessor tree not showing checked state**: `openDetail()` didn't call `setCheckedKeys()` on the tree. Fix: Added `nextTick` callback to set checked keys after dialog renders.
   - **Backend not restarted after new endpoint**: Backend was killed and restarted to load the new `/api/plans//copy` endpoint.

[181] 2026-06-02:
清单管理，分成两块，一个是镇街履职事项清单，一个是部门履职事项清单。
镇街履职事项清单分为基本履职事项、配合履职事项、上级部门收回事项。
部门履职事项清单分为基本履职事项、镇街配合履职事项、本部门收回事项。

镇街基本履职事项和部门基本履职事项是独立的，属于本单位职责事项，只需要序号和事项名称两个字段。
镇街的配合履职事项会同步到对应的部门，除了序号和事项名称以外，会有对应上级部门、上级部门职责和街道配合职责等字段。
部门的镇街配合履职事项不可编辑，会承接展示出镇街的配合履职事项数据。
镇街的上级部门收回事项也会同步到对应部门，除了序号和事项名称以外，会有承接部门、工作方式等字段。
部门的本部门收回事项不可编辑，会承接展示出镇街的上级部门收回事项数据。

   - ConfirmedPattern model for tracking confirmed false positives (now scoped to plan_id)
   - `existing_handled` set: preloads confirmed/resolved issues to skip re-detection
   - 4 detection engines: `_check_typos` (homophone/shape/year), `_check_logic` (numbering/contradiction/truncation/fuzzy), `_check_punctuation` (comma/bracket/missing end), `_check_duplicates` (Jaccard n-gram similarity)
   - `_hash_text()`: MD5 hashing for deterministic issue deduplication
   - `_load_confirmed_hashes(plan_id)`: loads ConfirmedPattern entries scoped to plan
   - `joinedload()`: SQLAlchemy eager loading to prevent N+1 queries
   - Client-side pagination via `pagedIssues` computed property
   - `loadId` counter for race condition protection in async operations
   - Chip/card filter pattern from Tasks.vue (chipCategory selector → chipOptions grid → toggleChip)
   - Vue 3 Composition API: ref, computed, reactive, watch
   - Axios interceptor: strips `.data` from response, so `r` is the body directly

     ```python
     class ConfirmedPattern(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "confirmed_pattern"
         id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
         plan_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("plan.id"), nullable=False, comment="所属方案ID")
         issue_type = db.Column(db.String(50), nullable=False, comment="问题分类")
         text_hash = db.Column(db.String(64), nullable=False, comment="文本 MD5")
         created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
         __table_args__ = (
             db.UniqueConstraint("plan_id", "issue_type", "text_hash", name="uq_confirmed_pattern_plan_type_hash"),
         )
     ```
     DB migration: `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS confirmed_pattern` then `db.create_all()` (table was empty, safe to drop).

         if (aa !== ba) return aa.localeCompare(ba, 'zh')

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **GBK encoding error with emoji**: `'gbk' codec can't encode character '\u2705'` — fixed by using ASCII-only output in Python scripts
   - **Duplicate Edit match in quality_check.py**: Two identical patterns (`filter(QualityIssue.status.in_(["confirmed", "resolved"]))`) in `list_managed_issues` and `list_grouped_issues` — fixed by providing more context (function signature) to disambiguate
   - **Edit string not found in QualityCheck.vue**: Comment text differed slightly between what was expected and actual file content — fixed by reading the file first to get exact text
   - **Summary counting bug (critical)**: `sum(1 for k, v in type_counts.items() if "错别字" in k)` was counting distinct ISSUE TYPE KEYS (e.g., 1 for "逻辑-编号混用") instead of total issues per category (e.g., 119 logic issues) — fixed by changing `sum(1 for ...)` to `sum(v for ...)` where `v` is the actual issue count. This was a long-standing bug that caused the summary cards to show "逻辑问题: 1" when there were actually 119 logic issues.
   - **GBK console encoding corruption**: Summary displays garbled Chinese characters in console output (e.g., '�߼�����' instead of '逻辑问题') — this is a display-only issue on Windows GBK consoles, does not affect functionality as confirmed by the numbers being correct (209 total, 119+90=209)

5. Problem Solving:
   - **240→210→209 root cause**: Confirmed as NOT a bug but a feature — ConfirmedPattern and existing_handled mechanisms intentionally skip previously handled issues. However, 3 bugs were found that could cause unexpected counting:

     2. text[:200] collision causing accidental dedup (fixed with MD5 hash)
     3. Non-deterministic duplicate detection (fixed with ORDER BY)
     4. Summary counting distinct types instead of total issues (fixed)

     ```python
     class StreetBasicItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "street_basic_item"
         id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
         unit_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("unit.id"), nullable=False)
         sort_order = db.Column(db.Integer, default=0)
         name = db.Column(db.String(500), nullable=False)
         created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", backref="street_basic_items")

     class StreetCoordinationItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "street_coordination_item"
         id, unit_id, sort_order, name, superior_dept_id, superior_dept_duty, street_duty, created_at
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[unit_id], backref="street_coordination_items")
         superior_dept = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[superior_dept_id])

     class StreetReclaimItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "street_reclaim_item"
         id, unit_id, sort_order, name, undertake_dept_id, work_method, created_at
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[unit_id], backref="street_reclaim_items")
         undertake_dept = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[undertake_dept_id])

     class DeptBasicItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "dept_basic_item"
         id, unit_id, sort_order, name, created_at
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", backref="dept_basic_items")
     ```

     ```python
     STREET_ROOT_NAMES = {"乡镇街道"}

     def _get_category_org(org):
         if org is None: return None
         # If current org's parent is root, return it
         if org.parent_id is not None:
             parent = Organization.query.get(org.parent_id)
             if parent is not None and parent.parent_id is None:
                 return org
         # Walk up to find category level (just below root)
         while org.parent_id is not None:
             parent = Organization.query.get(org.parent_id)
             if parent is None: break
             if parent.parent_id is None: return org
             org = parent
         return org

     def get_unit_type(unit):
         # Returns 'street' if org category is in STREET_ROOT_NAMES, else 'dept'

     def get_units_by_type(unit_type):
         # Returns [{id, name, org_id, org_name}, ...] for given type
     ```

   - Vue 3 Composition API: `ref`, `reactive`, `computed`, `watch`, `nextTick`
   - Element Plus `el-tree` with `setCheckedKeys()`, `getCheckedNodes()`, `getHalfCheckedNodes()`
   - CSS overflow detection: `scrollHeight > clientHeight` to determine if content overflows a single line

3. Files and Code Sections:

     - Template: Toggle moved before v-for, always rendered with `chip-toggle-hidden` class when not needed, removed `idx  setChipGridRef(dim.key, el)" :class="['chip-grid', { collapsed: dim.collapsed }]">
       {{ dim.collapsed ? '▼' : '▲' }}

     ```
     ```javascript
     const chipGridRefs = {}
     function setChipGridRef(key, el) { if (el) chipGridRefs[key] = el }
     function checkChipOverflow() {
       chipDimensions.forEach(d => d.collapsed = true)
       nextTick(() => {
         for (const dim of chipDimensions) {
           const el = chipGridRefs[dim.key]
           if (el) {
             const overflows = el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight + 2
             dim.needsToggle = overflows
             dim.collapsed = overflows
           }
         }
       })
     }
     ```
     ```css
     .chip-grid.collapsed { max-height: 30px; overflow-y: hidden; }
     .chip-toggle-hidden { visibility: hidden; pointer-events: none; }
     ```
     - Added `nextTick` import, `needsToggle: false` to each chip dimension
     - Calls `checkChipOverflow()` after `buildChipOptions()` in both `loadIssues()` and `runCheck()`

     - Same `checkChipOverflow()`, template, CSS changes

   - **`backend/routes/plan.py`**
     - Added `POST /api/plans//copy` endpoint (after line 184, `issue_plan`)
     - Copies plan config: creates new plan with name "原名称 - 副本" (with timestamp fallback for duplicates), copies evaluation_dimensions → assessment_dimensions (with `ad_id_map`), copies assessor_units, copies assessed_groups → units → dimension_weights (using ID mapping)
     ```python
     @plan_bp.route("/api/plans//copy", methods=["POST"])
     @jwt_required()
     def copy_plan(plan_id):
         src = Plan.query.get(plan_id)
         if not src: return jsonify({"msg": "方案不存在"}), 404
         copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本"
         existing = Plan.query.filter_by(name=copy_name).first()
         if existing: copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本({datetime.utcnow().strftime('%m%d%H%M')})"
         new_plan = Plan(name=copy_name, year=src.year, start_date=src.start_date,
                         end_date=src.end_date, created_date=datetime.utcnow().date(), status="draft")
         db.session.add(new_plan); db.session.flush()
         ad_id_map = {}
         for ed in src.evaluation_dimensions:
             new_ed = EvaluationDimension(name=ed.name, score=ed.score, is_actual_assessment=ed.is_actual_assessment,
                                          is_bonus_deduction=ed.is_bonus_deduction, sort_order=ed.sort_order, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_ed); db.session.flush()
             for ad in ed.assessment_dimensions:
                 new_ad = AssessmentDimension(name=ad.name, sort_order=ad.sort_order, evaluation_dimension_id=new_ed.id)
                 db.session.add(new_ad); db.session.flush()
                 ad_id_map[ad.id] = new_ad.id
         new_plan.assessor_units = src.assessor_units
         for g in src.assessed_groups:
             new_group = AssessedGroup(name=g.name, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_group); db.session.flush()
             new_group.units = g.units
             for gw in g.dimension_weights:
                 new_ad_id = ad_id_map.get(gw.assessment_dimension_id)
                 if new_ad_id:
                     db.session.add(GroupDimensionWeight(group_id=new_group.id, assessment_dimension_id=new_ad_id, weight=gw.weight))
         db.session.commit()
         return jsonify({"data": {"id": new_plan.id, "name": new_plan.name}, "msg": f"方案复制成功：{new_plan.name}"})
     ```

   - **`frontend/src/api/plan.js`**

     const checkedAssessorUnits = ref([])
     function onAssessorCheck(_node, info) {
       checkedAssessorUnits.value = [...info.checkedNodes, ...info.halfCheckedNodes].filter(n => n.type === 'unit')
     }
     ```

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Overflow detection always returning false**: `checkChipOverflow()` measured `scrollHeight > clientHeight` in the natural (non-collapsed) state where `max-height: 120px` fit all content. Fix: Force `collapsed = true` before measurement, then determine actual overflow.
   - **Class binding blocking collapsed style**: `{ collapsed: dim.needsToggle && dim.collapsed }` meant that when `needsToggle` was false (uninitialized), setting `collapsed = true` for measurement didn't apply the CSS class. Fix: Simplified to `{ collapsed: dim.collapsed }`.
   - **Second row peeking through**: `max-height: 36px` was too tall for one row (~28px chip height), allowing ~8px of second row visible. Fix: Reduced to `max-height: 30px`.
   - **Assessor tree not showing checked state**: `openDetail()` didn't call `setCheckedKeys()` on the tree. Fix: Added `nextTick` callback to set checked keys after dialog renders.
   - **Backend not restarted after new endpoint**: Backend was killed and restarted to load the new `/api/plans//copy` endpoint.

   - **backend/services/permission.py** (CREATED)
     - Core permission service with identity-based dynamic permissions
     - `ALL_PERM_CODES` = all 18 permission codes
     - `ASSESSOR_PERMS` = {task_create, task_distribute, task_review, task_score, quality_view, quality_manage}
     - `ASSESSED_PERMS` = {task_submit, checklist_edit, cadre_edit}
     - `get_user_permissions(user)`: returns role base permissions + identity overlay. is_system → all; else role perms + assessor/assessed overlay based on user.current_identity
     - `require_perm(*codes)`: decorator factory. Gets JWT identity → loads user → checks permission intersection → returns 403 if denied
     ```python
     def get_user_permissions(user):
         if user is None: return set()
         if user.role and user.role.is_system: return set(ALL_PERM_CODES)
         perms = set()
         if user.role:
             perms = {p.menu_code for p in user.role.permissions if p.can_access}
         if user.current_identity == "assessor": perms |= ASSESSOR_PERMS
         elif user.current_identity == "assessed": perms |= ASSESSED_PERMS
         return perms
     ```

   - Element Plus `el-tree` with `setCheckedKeys()`, `getCheckedNodes()`, `getHalfCheckedNodes()`
   - CSS overflow detection: `scrollHeight > clientHeight` to determine if content overflows a single line

     - Chip toggle rework (template + script + CSS)
     - Template: Toggle moved before v-for, always rendered with `chip-toggle-hidden` class when not needed, removed `idx  setChipGridRef(dim.key, el)" :class="['chip-grid', { collapsed: dim.collapsed }]">
       {{ dim.collapsed ? '▼' : '▲' }}
       暂无数据
       
         {{ item.name || item }}
       
     
     ```
     ```javascript
     const chipGridRefs = {}
     function setChipGridRef(key, el) { if (el) chipGridRefs[key] = el }
     function checkChipOverflow() {
       chipDimensions.forEach(d => d.collapsed = true)
       nextTick(() => {
         for (const dim of chipDimensions) {
           const el = chipGridRefs[dim.key]
           if (el) {
             const overflows = el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight + 2
             dim.needsToggle = overflows
             dim.collapsed = overflows
           }
         }
       })
     }
     ```
     ```css
     .chip-grid.collapsed { max-height: 30px; overflow-y: hidden; }
     .chip-toggle-hidden { visibility: hidden; pointer-events: none; }
     ```
     - Added `nextTick` import, `needsToggle: false` to each chip dimension
     - Calls `checkChipOverflow()` after `buildChipOptions()` in both `loadIssues()` and `runCheck()`

   - **`frontend/src/views/Tasks.vue`**
     - Identical chip toggle changes as QualityCheck.vue
     - Same `checkChipOverflow()`, template, CSS changes
     - `checkChipOverflow()` called after `buildChipOptions()` in `loadFilterOpts()`

   - **`backend/routes/plan.py`**
     - Added `POST /api/plans//copy` endpoint (after line 184, `issue_plan`)
     - Copies plan config: creates new plan with name "原名称 - 副本" (with timestamp fallback for duplicates), copies evaluation_dimensions → assessment_dimensions (with `ad_id_map`), copies assessor_units, copies assessed_groups → units → dimension_weights (using ID mapping)
     ```python
     @plan_bp.route("/api/plans//copy", methods=["POST"])
     @jwt_required()
     def copy_plan(plan_id):
         src = Plan.query.get(plan_id)
         if not src: return jsonify({"msg": "方案不存在"}), 404
         copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本"
         existing = Plan.query.filter_by(name=copy_name).first()
         if existing: copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本({datetime.utcnow().strftime('%m%d%H%M')})"
         new_plan = Plan(name=copy_name, year=src.year, start_date=src.start_date,
                         end_date=src.end_date, created_date=datetime.utcnow().date(), status="draft")
         db.session.add(new_plan); db.session.flush()
         ad_id_map = {}
         for ed in src.evaluation_dimensions:
             new_ed = EvaluationDimension(name=ed.name, score=ed.score, is_actual_assessment=ed.is_actual_assessment,
                                          is_bonus_deduction=ed.is_bonus_deduction, sort_order=ed.sort_order, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_ed); db.session.flush()
             for ad in ed.assessment_dimensions:
                 new_ad = AssessmentDimension(name=ad.name, sort_order=ad.sort_order, evaluation_dimension_id=new_ed.id)
                 db.session.add(new_ad); db.session.flush()
                 ad_id_map[ad.id] = new_ad.id
         new_plan.assessor_units = src.assessor_units
         for g in src.assessed_groups:
             new_group = AssessedGroup(name=g.name, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_group); db.session.flush()
             new_group.units = g.units
             for gw in g.dimension_weights:
                 new_ad_id = ad_id_map.get(gw.assessment_dimension_id)
                 if new_ad_id:
                     db.session.add(GroupDimensionWeight(group_id=new_group.id, assessment_dimension_id=new_ad_id, weight=gw.weight))
         db.session.commit()
         return jsonify({"data": {"id": new_plan.id, "name": new_plan.name}, "msg": f"方案复制成功：{new_plan.name}"})
     ```

   - **`frontend/src/api/plan.js`**

     ```javascript
     const checkedAssessorUnits = ref([])
     function onAssessorCheck(_node, info) {
       checkedAssessorUnits.value = [...info.checkedNodes, ...info.halfCheckedNodes].filter(n => n.type === 'unit')
     }
     ```

4. Errors and fixes:

   - **Class binding blocking collapsed style**: `{ collapsed: dim.needsToggle && dim.collapsed }` meant that when `needsToggle` was false (uninitialized), setting `collapsed = true` for measurement didn't apply the CSS class. Fix: Simplified to `{ collapsed: dim.collapsed }`.
   - **Second row peeking through**: `max-height: 36px` was too tall for one row (~28px chip height), allowing ~8px of second row visible. Fix: Reduced to `max-height: 30px`.
   - **Assessor tree not showing checked state**: `openDetail()` didn't call `setCheckedKeys()` on the tree. Fix: Added `nextTick` callback to set checked keys after dialog renders.
   - **Backend not restarted after new endpoint**: Backend was killed and restarted to load the new `/api/plans//copy` endpoint.

清单管理，分成两块，一个是镇街履职事项清单，一个是部门履职事项清单。
镇街履职事项清单分为基本履职事项、配合履职事项、上级部门收回事项。
部门履职事项清单分为基本履职事项、镇街配合履职事项、本部门收回事项。

镇街基本履职事项和部门基本履职事项是独立的，属于本单位职责事项，只需要序号和事项名称两个字段。

部门的镇街配合履职事项不可编辑，会承接展示出镇街的配合履职事项数据。
镇街的上级部门收回事项也会同步到对应部门，除了序号和事项名称以外，会有承接部门、工作方式等字段。
部门的本部门收回事项不可编辑，会承接展示出镇街的上级部门收回事项数据。

     class StreetBasicItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "street_basic_item"
         id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
         unit_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("unit.id"), nullable=False)
         sort_order = db.Column(db.Integer, default=0)
         name = db.Column(db.String(500), nullable=False)
         created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", backref="street_basic_items")

     class StreetCoordinationItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "street_coordination_item"
         id, unit_id, sort_order, name, superior_dept_id, superior_dept_duty, street_duty, created_at
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[unit_id], backref="street_coordination_items")
         superior_dept = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[superior_dept_id])

     class StreetReclaimItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "street_reclaim_item"
         id, unit_id, sort_order, name, undertake_dept_id, work_method, created_at
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[unit_id], backref="street_reclaim_items")
         undertake_dept = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[undertake_dept_id])

     class DeptBasicItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "dept_basic_item"
         id, unit_id, sort_order, name, created_at
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", backref="dept_basic_items")
     ```

     ```python
     STREET_ROOT_NAMES = {"乡镇街道"}

     def _get_category_org(org):
         if org is None: return None
         # If current org's parent is root, return it
         if org.parent_id is not None:
             parent = Organization.query.get(org.parent_id)
             if parent is not None and parent.parent_id is None:
                 return org
         # Walk up to find category level (just below root)
         while org.parent_id is not None:
             parent = Organization.query.get(org.parent_id)
             if parent is None: break
             if parent.parent_id is None: return org
             org = parent
         return org

     def get_unit_type(unit):
         # Returns 'street' if org category is in STREET_ROOT_NAMES, else 'dept'

     def get_units_by_type(unit_type):
         # Returns [{id, name, org_id, org_name}, ...] for given type
     ```

       if (!COLUMN_TO_FIELD[col]) return false
       if ((issue.issue_type || '').includes('错别字')) return !!parseSuggestion(issue.suggestion || '')

[330] 2026-06-02:

[331] 2026-06-02:
和你确认一下，重复任务的校验。

1.不同主考单位对不同被考核单位下的相同或相似任务
2.不同主考单位对同一被考核单位下的相同或相似任务
3.同一主考单位对同一被考核单位下的相同或相似任务
以上三种情况都会视为重复任务。

同一主考单位对不同被考核单位下的相同或相似任务，这种情况不会视为重复任务。
================================================================================

防止以指标考指标的逻辑不对，他的正确逻辑是，某个主考单位自己有一条或多条指标类任务，他需要完成这条任务，需要摊派下给别的单位（镇街和部门），但依然使用指标类任务进行下派，未对这一类任务的实际完成做要求，以指标考指标。
比如：区发改委给区文旅委下派了“规上文化、体育和娱乐业营业收入增速（错月）达到20%以上。和文化、体育和娱乐业工资总额增速达到12%以上。”这两条任务，区文旅委转头给乡镇下了“对文化体育娱乐业经济增长支撑达12%及以上。”任务。还给部门下派了“旅游零售业增加值同比增长20%。
和旅游住宿和餐饮业增加值同比增长20%。”等任务，这种就是以指标考核指标。

放宽后的阈值，可以参考我举的例子，调一个合理的值。

用“疑似以指标考指标"的标签统一展示，"指标类任务"的标签不再展示。

不入库、不计入问题总数。

       {{ dim.collapsed ? '▼' : '▲' }}

         {{ item.name || item }}
       
     
     ```
     ```javascript
     const chipGridRefs = {}
     function setChipGridRef(key, el) { if (el) chipGridRefs[key] = el }
     function checkChipOverflow() {
       chipDimensions.forEach(d => d.collapsed = true)
       nextTick(() => {
         for (const dim of chipDimensions) {
           const el = chipGridRefs[dim.key]
           if (el) {
             const overflows = el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight + 2
             dim.needsToggle = overflows
             dim.collapsed = overflows
           }
         }
       })
     }
     ```
     ```css
     .chip-grid.collapsed { max-height: 30px; overflow-y: hidden; }
     .chip-toggle-hidden { visibility: hidden; pointer-events: none; }
     ```
     - Added `nextTick` import, `needsToggle: false` to each chip dimension
     - Calls `checkChipOverflow()` after `buildChipOptions()` in both `loadIssues()` and `runCheck()`

   - **`frontend/src/views/Tasks.vue`**
     - Identical chip toggle changes as QualityCheck.vue
     - Same `checkChipOverflow()`, template, CSS changes
     - `checkChipOverflow()` called after `buildChipOptions()` in `loadFilterOpts()`

   - **`backend/routes/plan.py`**
     - Added `POST /api/plans//copy` endpoint (after line 184, `issue_plan`)
     - Copies plan config: creates new plan with name "原名称 - 副本" (with timestamp fallback for duplicates), copies evaluation_dimensions → assessment_dimensions (with `ad_id_map`), copies assessor_units, copies assessed_groups → units → dimension_weights (using ID mapping)
     ```python
     @plan_bp.route("/api/plans//copy", methods=["POST"])
     @jwt_required()
     def copy_plan(plan_id):
         src = Plan.query.get(plan_id)
         if not src: return jsonify({"msg": "方案不存在"}), 404
         copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本"
         existing = Plan.query.filter_by(name=copy_name).first()
         if existing: copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本({datetime.utcnow().strftime('%m%d%H%M')})"
         new_plan = Plan(name=copy_name, year=src.year, start_date=src.start_date,
                         end_date=src.end_date, created_date=datetime.utcnow().date(), status="draft")
         db.session.add(new_plan); db.session.flush()
         ad_id_map = {}
         for ed in src.evaluation_dimensions:
             new_ed = EvaluationDimension(name=ed.name, score=ed.score, is_actual_assessment=ed.is_actual_assessment,
                                          is_bonus_deduction=ed.is_bonus_deduction, sort_order=ed.sort_order, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_ed); db.session.flush()
             for ad in ed.assessment_dimensions:
                 new_ad = AssessmentDimension(name=ad.name, sort_order=ad.sort_order, evaluation_dimension_id=new_ed.id)
                 db.session.add(new_ad); db.session.flush()
                 ad_id_map[ad.id] = new_ad.id
         new_plan.assessor_units = src.assessor_units
         for g in src.assessed_groups:
             new_group = AssessedGroup(name=g.name, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_group); db.session.flush()
             new_group.units = g.units
             for gw in g.dimension_weights:
                 new_ad_id = ad_id_map.get(gw.assessment_dimension_id)
                 if new_ad_id:
                     db.session.add(GroupDimensionWeight(group_id=new_group.id, assessment_dimension_id=new_ad_id, weight=gw.weight))
         db.session.commit()
         return jsonify({"data": {"id": new_plan.id, "name": new_plan.name}, "msg": f"方案复制成功：{new_plan.name}"})
     ```

   - **`frontend/src/api/plan.js`**

     class StreetBasicItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "street_basic_item"
         id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
         unit_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("unit.id"), nullable=False)
         sort_order = db.Column(db.Integer, default=0)
         name = db.Column(db.String(500), nullable=False)
         created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", backref="street_basic_items")

     class StreetCoordinationItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "street_coordination_item"
         id, unit_id, sort_order, name, superior_dept_id, superior_dept_duty, street_duty, created_at
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[unit_id], backref="street_coordination_items")
         superior_dept = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[superior_dept_id])

     class StreetReclaimItem(db.Model):
         __tablename__ = "street_reclaim_item"
         id, unit_id, sort_order, name, undertake_dept_id, work_method, created_at
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", foreign_keys=[unit_id], backref="street_reclaim_items")

     ```python
     STREET_ROOT_NAMES = {"乡镇街道"}

     def _get_category_org(org):
         if org is None: return None
         # If current org's parent is root, return it
         if org.parent_id is not None:
             parent = Organization.query.get(org.parent_id)
             if parent is not None and parent.parent_id is None:
                 return org
         # Walk up to find category level (just below root)
         while org.parent_id is not None:
             parent = Organization.query.get(org.parent_id)
             if parent is None: break
             if parent.parent_id is None: return org
             org = parent
         return org

     def get_unit_type(unit):
         # Returns 'street' if org category is in STREET_ROOT_NAMES, else 'dept'

     def get_units_by_type(unit_type):
         # Returns [{id, name, org_id, org_name}, ...] for given type
     ```

   - **backend/services/permission.py** (CREATED)
     - Core permission service with identity-based dynamic permissions
     - `ALL_PERM_CODES` = all 18 permission codes
     - `ASSESSOR_PERMS` = {task_create, task_distribute, task_review, task_score, quality_view, quality_manage}
     - `ASSESSED_PERMS` = {task_submit, checklist_edit, cadre_edit}
     - `get_user_permissions(user)`: returns role base permissions + identity overlay. is_system → all; else role perms + assessor/assessed overlay based on user.current_identity
     - `require_perm(*codes)`: decorator factory. Gets JWT identity → loads user → checks permission intersection → returns 403 if denied
     ```python
     def get_user_permissions(user):
         if user is None: return set()
         if user.role and user.role.is_system: return set(ALL_PERM_CODES)
         perms = set()
         if user.role:
             perms = {p.menu_code for p in user.role.permissions if p.can_access}
         if user.current_identity == "assessor": perms |= ASSESSOR_PERMS
         elif user.current_identity == "assessed": perms |= ASSESSED_PERMS
         return perms
     ```

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user made multiple iterative requests to improve the quality check system's duplicate detection and metric task detection:

   - **Duplicate detection optimization**: Fix false positives where 重点工作 matched but 主要任务 was completely different. Expand scope to cross-unit comparison. After initial changes caused all duplicates to disappear, relax thresholds.
   
   - **"疑��以指标考指标" detection**: Replace simple "指标类任务" flagging with intelligent detection of units that receive percentage-based metric tasks from above and pass semantically similar metric tasks to subordinate units without decomposing them into operational work. Algorithm: find middle units that both receive AND assign metric tasks, pairwise compare received vs assigned using specialized n-gram (preserving digits and %), threshold ≥ 0.20.
   
   - **Correction on "一级单位" definition**: Initially interpreted as township/street units, user corrected that it means the middle unit (first receiver, like 区文旅委) — removed the wrong street filter.
   
   - **Confirmed state persistence**: Confirmed proxy pairs saved to ConfirmedPattern, persist across re-checks, sort to end with gray text.
   
   - **List grouping**: Merge issues in the QC list by (assessor, main_task) into one row per task group, while keeping individual issue counts.
   
   - **Edit dialog unit tabs**: Show assessed units as tabs in edit dialog, switch to edit each unit's task. "保存并更正" → "保存并更正当前" (only current unit).

2. Key Technical Concepts:

         __tablename__ = "confirmed_pattern"
         id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
