   - **Summary counting bug (critical)**: `sum(1 for k, v in type_counts.items() if "错别字" in k)` was counting distinct ISSUE TYPE KEYS (e.g., 1 for "逻辑-编号混用") instead of total issues per category (e.g., 119 logic issues) — fixed by changing `sum(1 for ...)` to `sum(v for ...)` where `v` is the actual issue count. 5. Problem Solving:
   - **240→210→209 root cause**: Confirmed as NOT a bug but a feature — ConfirmedPattern and existing_handled mechanisms intentionally skip previously handled issues. However, 3 bugs were found that could cause unexpected counting:
     1. Cross-plan ConfirmedPattern pollution (fixed with plan_id scoping)
     2. text[:200] collision causing accidental dedup (fixed with MD5 hash)
     3. Non-deterministic duplicate detection (fixed with ORDER BY)
     4. Summary counting distinct types instead of total issues (fixed)
   - Added transparency: `excluded.by_confirmed_pattern` and `excluded.by_existing_handled` in API response
   - **Deterministic verification**: E2E test confirmed 2 consecutive runs produce exactly 209 issues (was non-deterministic before ORDER BY fix)

   - **Add selected assessor unit list**: Below the tree component, show a tag list of all currently checked/selected assessor units for quick visual reference.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Vue 3 Composition API: `ref`, `reactive`, `computed`, `watch`, `nextTick`
   - Element Plus `el-tree` with `setCheckedKeys()`, `getCheckedNodes()`, `getHalfCheckedNodes()`
   - CSS overflow detection: `scrollHeight > clientHeight` to determine if content overflows a single line
   - `visibility: hidden` (not `display: none`) to preserve element space for alignment
   - `nextTick` for DOM-dependent measurements after reactive data changes
   - Flask + SQLAlchemy: copying DB records with ID mapping for foreign key relationships
   - CSS `max-height` + `overflow: hidden` for chip row collapsing

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`frontend/src/views/QualityCheck.vue`**
     - Chip toggle rework (template + script + CSS)
     - Template: Toggle moved before v-for, always rendered with `chip-toggle-hidden` class when not needed, removed `idx  setChipGridRef(dim.key, el)" :class="['chip-grid', { collapsed: dim.collapsed }]">
       {{ dim.collapsed ? '▼' : '▲' }}
       暂无数据
       
         {{ item.name || item }}
       
     
     ```
     ```javascript
     const chipGridRefs = {}
     function setChipGridRef(key, el) { if (el) chipGridRefs[key] = el }
     function checkChipOverflow() {
       chipDimensions.forEach(d => d.collapsed = true)
       nextTick(() => {
         for (const dim of chipDimensions) {
           const el = chipGridRefs[dim.key]
           if (el) {
             const overflows = el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight + 2
             dim.needsToggle = overflows
             dim.collapsed = overflows
           }
         }
       })
     }
     ```
     ```css
     .chip-grid.collapsed { max-height: 30px; overflow-y: hidden; }
     .chip-toggle-hidden { visibility: hidden; pointer-events: none; }
     ```
     - Added `nextTick` import, `needsToggle: false` to each chip dimension
     - Calls `checkChipOverflow()` after `buildChipOptions()` in both `loadIssues()` and `runCheck()`

   - **`frontend/src/views/Tasks.vue`**
     - Identical chip toggle changes as QualityCheck.vue
     - Same `checkChipOverflow()`, template, CSS changes
     - `checkChipOverflow()` called after `buildChipOptions()` in `loadFilterOpts()`

   - **`backend/routes/plan.py`**
     - Added `POST /api/plans//copy` endpoint (after line 184, `issue_plan`)
     - Copies plan config: creates new plan with name "原名称 - 副本" (with timestamp fallback for duplicates), copies evaluation_dimensions → assessment_dimensions (with `ad_id_map`), copies assessor_units, copies assessed_groups → units → dimension_weights (using ID mapping)
     ```python
     @plan_bp.route("/api/plans//copy", methods=["POST"])
     @jwt_required()
     def copy_plan(plan_id):
         src = Plan.query.get(plan_id)
         if not src: return jsonify({"msg": "方案不存在"}), 404
         copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本"
         existing = Plan.query.filter_by(name=copy_name).first()
         if existing: copy_name = f"{src.name} - 副本({datetime.utcnow().strftime('%m%d%H%M')})"
         new_plan = Plan(name=copy_name, year=src.year, start_date=src.start_date,
                         end_date=src.end_date, created_date=datetime.utcnow().date(), status="draft")
         db.session.add(new_plan); db.session.flush()
         ad_id_map = {}
         for ed in src.evaluation_dimensions:
             new_ed = EvaluationDimension(name=ed.name, score=ed.score, is_actual_assessment=ed.is_actual_assessment,
                                          is_bonus_deduction=ed.is_bonus_deduction, sort_order=ed.sort_order, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_ed); db.session.flush()
             for ad in ed.assessment_dimensions:
                 new_ad = AssessmentDimension(name=ad.name, sort_order=ad.sort_order, evaluation_dimension_id=new_ed.id)
                 db.session.add(new_ad); db.session.flush()
                 ad_id_map[ad.id] = new_ad.id
         new_plan.assessor_units = src.assessor_units
         for g in src.assessed_groups:
             new_group = AssessedGroup(name=g.name, plan_id=new_plan.id)
             db.session.add(new_group); db.session.flush()
             new_group.units = g.units
             for gw in g.dimension_weights:
                 new_ad_id = ad_id_map.get(gw.assessment_dimension_id)
                 if new_ad_id:
                     db.session.add(GroupDimensionWeight(group_id=new_group.id, assessment_dimension_id=new_ad_id, weight=gw.weight))
         db.session.commit()
         return jsonify({"data": {"id": new_plan.id, "name": new_plan.name}, "msg": f"方案复制成功：{new_plan.name}"})
     ```

   - **`frontend/src/api/plan.js`**
     - Added `export function copyPlan(id) { return http.post(`/plans/${id}/copy`) }`

   - **`frontend/src/views/Plans.vue`**
     - Added "复制" button in operations column (green, `type="success"`, between 下发 and 删除)

     - Added `checkedAssessorUnits` ref and tag list display below tree
     - Updated `onAssessorCheck()` to collect checked + half-checked unit nodes
     ```javascript
     const checkedAssessorUnits = ref([])
     function onAssessorCheck(_node, info) {
       checkedAssessorUnits.value = [...info.checkedNodes, ...info.halfCheckedNodes].filter(n => n.type === 'unit')
     }
     ```
     - Template addition below tree:
     ```html

       {{ u.name }}
     
     ```
     - `openDetail()` and `refreshDetail()` also initialize `checkedAssessorUnits.value = detail.value.assessor_units`

   - **Class binding blocking collapsed style**: `{ collapsed: dim.needsToggle && dim.collapsed }` meant that when `needsToggle` was false (uninitialized), setting `collapsed = true` for measurement didn't apply the CSS class. Fix: Simplified to `{ collapsed: dim.collapsed }`.
   - **Second row peeking through**: `max-height: 36px` was too tall for one row (~28px chip height), allowing ~8px of second row visible. Fix: Reduced to `max-height: 30px`.

   - **Backend not restarted after new endpoint**: Backend was killed and restarted to load the new `/api/plans//copy` endpoint.

清单管理，分成两块，一个是镇街履职事项清单，一个是部门履职事项清单。
镇街履职事项清单分为基本履职事项、配合履职事项、上级部门收回事项。
部门履职事项清单分为基本履职事项、镇街配合履职事项、本部门收回事项。

镇街基本履职事项和部门基本履职事项是独立的，属于本单位职责事项，只需要序号和事项名称两个字段。
镇街的配合履职事项会同步到对应的部门，除了序号和事项名称以外，会有对应上级部门、上级部门职责和街道配合职责等字段。
部门的镇街配合履职事项不可编辑，会承接展示出镇街的配合履职事项数据。
镇街的上级部门收回事项也会同步到对应部门，除了序号和事项名称以外，会有承接部门、工作方式等字段。
部门的本部门收回事项不可编辑，会承接展示出镇街的上级部门收回事项数据。

         name = db.Column(db.String(500), nullable=False)
         created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
         unit = db.relationship("Unit", backref="street_basic_items")

     class StreetCoordinationItem(db.Model):

     STREET_ROOT_NAMES = {"乡镇街道"}

     def _get_category_org(org):
         if org is None: return None
         # If current org's parent is root, return it
         if org.parent_id is not None:
             parent = Organization.query.get(org.parent_id)
             if parent is not None and parent.parent_id is None:
                 return org
         # Walk up to find category level (just below root)
         while org.parent_id is not None:
             parent = Organization.query.get(org.parent_id)
             if parent is None: break
             if parent.parent_id is None: return org
             org = parent
         return org

     def get_unit_type(unit):

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested a comprehensive role permission refinement across multiple iterations:

   **Phase 1 — Initial Permission System:**
   - User defined three roles: 发布单位 (highest authority), 主考单位 (assessor: receive plans, receive tasks, distribute tasks, view, review, score), 被考核单位 (assessed: receive plans/tasks, fill/report tasks, bind checklist items, associate cadres, process tasks)

   - Actually enforce permissions on backend (previously only @jwt_required was used, no role checking)
   - Add frontend permission-based menu/button visibility

   **Phase 2 — Missing Roles Fix:**
   - User reported: "目前角色管理里面只有发布单位，没有主考单位和被考核单位？"

   **Phase 3 — Plan-Driven Dynamic Permissions (current model):**
   - User clarified: "区委考核专班的角色为发布单位。其他单位登录后需选择考核方案。在考核方案中被分到主考单位或被考核单位的，赋予相应权限。可以同时拥有两种权限，拥有两种权限的单位可以进行角色切换。"
   - Fundamentally changed model: 发布单位 is the ONLY fixed role (for 区委考核专班). All other units get a basic "普通单位" role, and their assessor/assessed permissions are dynamically derived from their participation in the currently selected 考核方案 (plan).

   Earlier in this session:
   - "单位管理里面的标签需要把单位的名称显示全" — Show full unit names in tree tags
   - "首页改为驾驶舱" — Rename homepage to 驾驶舱

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Flask + JWT authentication with `@jwt_required()` decorator
   - Flask Blueprint-based route organization
   - SQLAlchemy ORM with SQLite (Role, RolePermission, User, Plan, Task models)
   - Vue 3 Composition API with Pinia store
   - Element Plus UI components (el-select, el-tag, el-menu, el-tree-select)
   - Plan-driven dynamic permission model: base permissions from role + identity-based overlay
   - `current_identity` field on User model (assessor/assessed) for data scoping
   - `build_task_query()` filters tasks by user.unit_id based on identity
   - `Plan.assessor_units` (many-to-many via plan_unit) and `Plan.assessed_groups[i].units` (many-to-many via group_unit)
   - Custom `@require_perm(*codes)` decorator with intersection check
   - `is_system` flag on Role grants all permissions

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **backend/app.py** — `_seed_default_admin()` function
     - Critical: This function seeds preset roles. Changed from 3 roles (发布单位/主考单位/被考核单位) to 2 roles (发布单位/普通单位). Now uses incremental creation.
     - 发布单位: is_system=True, all 18 permission codes
     - 普通单位: is_system=False, 5 basic codes: dashboard, plan_view, task_view, checklist_view, cadre_view
     - Also migrates users from old role names to new ones.
     ```python
     role_perms = {
         "发布单位": (all_codes, True),
         "普通单位": (basic_codes, False),
     }
     ```
     - Incremental: checks `Role.query.filter_by(name=name).first()` — if exists, updates is_system flag and adds missing perms; if not, creates.
     - Migration: moves users from old "系统管理员"/"主考单位"/"被考核单位" roles to new roles.

   - **backend/services/permission.py** (CREATED)
     - Core permission service with identity-based dynamic permissions
     - `ALL_PERM_CODES` = all 18 permission codes
     - `ASSESSOR_PERMS` = {task_create, task_distribute, task_review, task_score, quality_view, quality_manage}
     - `ASSESSED_PERMS` = {task_submit, checklist_edit, cadre_edit}
     - `get_user_permissions(user)`: returns role base permissions + identity overlay. is_system → all; else role perms + assessor/assessed overlay based on user.current_identity
     - `require_perm(*codes)`: decorator factory. Gets JWT identity → loads user → checks permission intersection → returns 403 if denied
     ```python
     def get_user_permissions(user):
         if user is None: return set()
         if user.role and user.role.is_system: return set(ALL_PERM_CODES)
         perms = set()
         if user.role:
             perms = {p.menu_code for p in user.role.permissions if p.can_access}
         if user.current_identity == "assessor": perms |= ASSESSOR_PERMS
         elif user.current_identity == "assessed": perms |= ASSESSED_PERMS
         return perms
     ```

   - **backend/routes/auth.py**
     - Added `from services.permission import get_user_permissions` import
     - `get_me()`: now returns `permissions` dict and `role_is_system` boolean
     - `_get_plan_identities(user, plan_id)`: helper that checks if user's unit is in plan.assessor_units and/or plan.assessed_groups[i].units. is_system users get both identities.
     - `GET /api/auth/plan-identities?plan_id=X` (NEW): returns `{is_assessor, is_assessed, identities: [...]}`
     - `switch_identity()` (REWRITTEN): non-system users must provide plan_id and can only switch between available identities. System users can freely toggle. Returns updated permissions.

     async function setPlan(planId) {
       currentPlanId.value = planId
       if (!planId) { availableIdentities.value = []; return }
       const res = await http.get('/auth/plan-identities', { params: { plan_id: planId } })
       availableIdentities.value = (res.data || res).identities || []
       if (availableIdentities.value.length === 1 && currentIdentity.value !== availableIdentities.value[0]) {
         await _doSwitchIdentity(availableIdentities.value[0])
       }
     }
     ```

   - **frontend/src/api/auth.js** — Updated `switchIdentityApi` to accept planId parameter:
     ```javascript
     export function switchIdentityApi(planId) {
       return http.put('/auth/identity', { plan_id: planId || null })
     }
     ```

   - **frontend/src/views/Layout.vue** — Major changes:
     - Sidebar: all menu items have `v-if="auth.hasPerm('...')"` for permission-based visibility
     - `` only shown if any child is visible (`v-if="auth.hasAnyPerm(...)"`)
     - Header-left: Plan selector dropdown for non-system users + optimized identity tag:
       - System user: shows "发布单位 · 最高权限" red tag
       - Single identity: shows non-clickable label ("主考单位" or "被考核单位")
       - Dual identity: shows clickable switch tag with Switch icon

     - Script: Added `loadPlanList()`, `onPlanChange()`, auto-selects latest plan on mount for non-system users

     ```

   - **frontend/src/views/Tasks.vue** — Key changes:

     - Action column width: `auth.hasAnyPerm('task_create','task_review','task_score') ? 280 : 200`
     - Bidirectional plan sync: `filterPlanId` watches `auth.currentPlanId` and vice versa

     watch(filterPlanId, async (pid) => {
       if (pid && pid !== auth.currentPlanId) { auth.setPlan(pid) }
       // ... load plan details
     })
     watch(() => auth.currentPlanId, (pid) => {
       if (pid && pid !== filterPlanId.value) { filterPlanId.value = pid }
     })
     ```

         return jsonify({"msg": "用户不存在"}), 401
     if password and not user.check_password(password):
         return jsonify({"msg": "密码错误"}), 401

     # Quick-switch: accepts target_username for reverse switch
     @auth_bp.route("/api/auth/quick-switch", methods=["POST"])
     @jwt_required()
     def quick_switch():
         # Mode 1: switch to specific username
         if target_username:
             target_user = User.query.filter_by(username=target_username).first()
             ...
         # Mode 2: switch by unit name (system users only)

       const col = issue.column_name || issue.column || ''
       if (!COLUMN_TO_FIELD[col]) return false
       if ((issue.issue_type || '').includes('错别字')) return !!parseSuggestion(issue.suggestion || '')
       if ((issue.issue_type || '').includes('标点')) {
         return ['英文逗号', '英文括号', '缺少结尾'].some(t => (issue.issue_type || '').includes(t))

[495] 2026-06-02:
和你确认一下，重复任务的校验。

1.不同主考单位对不同被考核单位下的相同或相似任务
2.不同主考单位对同一被考核单位下的相同或相似任务
3.同一主考单位对同一被考核单位下的相同或相似任务
以上三种情况都会视为重复任务。

同一主考单位对不同被考核单位下的相同或相似任务，这种情况不会视为重复任务。

     function hasPerm(permCode) {
       if (!userInfo.value) return false
       if (userInfo.value.role?.is_system) return true
       const perms = userInfo.value.role?.permissions || []
       return perms.some(p => p.can_access && p.menu_code === permCode)
     }
